Better: Use dykstra projection and smooth gradient. At each iteration:
  compute c = correlate(h,1-h)*dx
  weights = softmax(α(c - maxc))
  grad = dx * sum_k weights_k ∂c_k/∂h
  update z = h - step * (grad - mean(grad)) to keep sum approximately (subtract mean enforces zero sum) then project to [0,1] with sum n/2. This is projected gradient (subgradient) for nonconvex objective but smooth approx. The mean subtraction handles equality Lagrange multiplier approx. Then restart α? Let's implement fast exact c evaluation:
def correlate_c_present(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
def c5(h,n): return float(np.max(correlate...)* (2.0/n))

Detailed projected Adam algorithm:
def smooth_obj_grad(h,n,alpha): uses cvals, computes obj maybe not needed for gradient? For line search we may use exact. Return grad.
Pseudo:
 c = np.correlate(h,1-h)*dx (full vals)
 m = c.max()
 shifted = alpha*(c-m)
 # avoid over/under: shifted max 0, if alpha huge can underflow; use max shifted.
 mx = shifted.max() # =0 if m present. weights = np.exp(shifted-mx); sumw.
 grad = zeros
 loop d as above with weights[d_index]
 grad *= dx
 grad -= grad.mean()  # enforce sum zero direction
 return grad, maybe weights
But if alpha very large, weights concentrated; logsumexp objective gradient. For max, use weights among near max. Good.
Adam state: m,v. Step lr. For each iter:

 m_t = β1 m + (1-β1)g; v...
 step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
 Ensure zero mean step? Projected gradient direction should zero mean (gradient mean subtracted); Adam may introduce nonzero due state? Subtract mean after update.
 h_new = project(h - step)
 Evaluate exact C5; accept if lower? Since nonconvex, use line search: try step, if exact C5 not lower, halve step up to 10; accept best. This can be slow but safe.

Move types:
- Single coordinate change with sum correction? Could degrade sum. Better pair swap: choose p,q, exchange values? If h[p]!=h[q], swapping preserves sum, bounds. Let's derive update for a set of variables S with old values and new values. For each lag l, old c_l = sum terms. If variables p and q, only terms where i or i-d is p/q change. Let old p value a, new a+δ; q old b, new b-δ (transfer sum). Define indicator for p and q.
For a lag l (d): old contribution involving variables:
Terms:
- If i=p and p-d=q? product h_p(1-h_q): old a(1-b). More generally, each variable occurrence as first or second. We can compute old_contrib for p/q by checking up to 4 index equalities: first index p or q; second index p or q. Then compute old term contributions from variables if present, subtract from old c, add new terms.
For each lag, to update delta, we need know at most:

Implement projected gradient (PPP) with exact line search in detail.

Function smooth_gradient(h, n, alpha):
- compute c = np.correlate(h, 1-h)*dx (full scaled)
- m=c.max()
- # stabilize if alpha large
 y=alpha*(c-m)
 # y max 0, min very negative. If overflow? max 0 so exp≤1, no overflow. sumw = np.exp(y).sum(); if sumw==0? at max gives 1.
 w = np.exp(y)/sumw
- grad zeros
- Loop d index: for idx,w in enumerate(w): if w>1e-12: d=idx-(n-1). Use formulas.
Because w length L=2n-1 ~1400, loop per gradient. Vector operations length n. Use local variables for speed.

Projection function: Need fast. We can maybe use scipy.optimize? Implement bisection.
def project_feasible(v, n): target=n/2.
  v=np.asarray(v,float)
  # since target average=0.5, threshold can be bounded min(v)-1, max(v)+1
  lo=v.min()-1.0; hi=v.max()+1.0
  # If v close to target, fewer iterations
  for 40:
   tau=(lo+hi)*0.5
   s=np.clip(v-tau,0,1).sum()
   if s>target: lo=tau else hi=tau
  h=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>0:
    interior=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
    if interior.any(): h[interior]+=diff/interior.sum()
    else: # rare, adjust largest room? 
  # clip again tiny
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # Adjust sum exactly to n/2 as close as possible by distributing remaining over entries with room sorted? Could ignore.
  return h
But if diff after clipping > tolerance due threshold? Diff should small. For exactness, we can do a final correction:
  diff = target - h.sum()
  if diff !=0:
    if diff>0: add to entries <1 in chunks of min(1-h); else subtract to >0.
    Use sorted? Since diff likely <1e-10, just add to interior.

  h=np.clip(v,0,1)
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-13:
    # if adding, add proportionally to room? simple h += diff/n, then if violations clip iteratively few
  for _ in range(5): h=np.clip(h,0,1); diff=target-h.sum(); if small break; if diff>0 add to 1-h proportional; else subtract proportional to h.
This is O(n). Good. Use this frequent; use full project for initial.
But near boundaries, simple all add may violate; use proportional:

Adam step:
def adam_step(h, grad, state, lr, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
 m = state['m']; v=state['v']; t=state['t']+1
 m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
 v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*(grad*grad)
 mhat=m/(1-beta1**t); vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
 update = lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
 # ensure zero mean? grad already mean0, but update maybe not; subtract mean
 update -= update.mean()
 h_new = project_fast(h + update, n)
 return h_new, state

Implementation details:
def next_pow2(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
def full_corr_scaled(h,n):
  g = 1.0 - h
  L = 2*n-1
  N = 1 << (L-1).bit_length()
  fg_rev = np.fft.rfft(g[::-1], N)
  fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
  c = np.fft.irfft(fh*fg_rev, N)[:L]
  # sym tiny? return c * (2.0/n)
But computing g[::-1] each time. Could compute g; g_rev = g[::-1]. Fine.

- In line search, we only need exact and maybe smooth. exact can use fast_corr_scaled; smooth uses another FFT (same). Could write function evaluate(h,n,alpha): returns exact, smooth, c. It will FFT g etc.

Gradient function using FFT:
def smooth_grad(h,n,alpha):
  dx=2.0/n; L=2*n-1
  N=next_pow2(L)
  fh = np.fft.rfft(h,N)
  g=1-h
  fg = np.fft.rfft(g,N)
  fgr = np.fft.rfft(g[::-1],N)
  c = np.fft.irfft(fh*fgr,N)[:L] * dx
  m=c.max()
  y = alpha*(c-m)
  e = np.exp(y)  # max 1
  s = e.sum()
  w = e/s
  # A conv w with g: Wfull length L. Need Fw = rfft(w,N)
  fw = np.fft.rfft(w,N)
  A = np.fft.irfft(fw*fg,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
  wn = w[::-1]
  fwn = np.fft.rfft(wn,N)
  B = np.fft.irfft(fwn*fh,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
  grad = dx*(A - B)
  grad -= grad.mean()
  obj = m + np.log(s)/alpha
  return obj, grad, c, m
Check: w length L padded to N; convolution A = w * g, slice n-1:2n-1. Irfft length N. Good.
Potential numerical: A,B may have tiny noise. OK.
But is c exact with FFT orientation? Need ensure fast_corr_scaled uses same as np.correlate. Let's test small mentally with n=2. h=[.6,.4], g=[.4,.6], g_rev=[.6,.4]. conv h*g_rev: [.36, .48, .16]. c vals dx=1. c = [0.36,0.48,0.16]. Good.
Gradient at alpha large weights concentrated at d=0. w=[~0,1,0]. Then A slice? W=[0,1,0]. conv with g: [0,g0,g1,0], slice 1:2 -> [g0,g1]=1-h. B conv Wneg same [0,1,0] with h: [h0,h1]. grad=1-2h. Correct derivative c0.
Great.

      for _ in range(12):

         f_trial, obj_trial, _ = evaluate Smooth? We can call smooth_grad but expensive. Could exact only.

def adam_opt(h,n,alphas,passes,lr_base,deadline,rng,noise_level):
 current = h.copy(); current_c = c5_value(current,n)

Project fast implementation:
```
def project_fast(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += diff * room / tot
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += diff * h / tot
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final fine correction
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        interior = (h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        if interior.any():
            h[interior] += diff / interior.sum()
            h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This may not exactly sum after final clip. Could run one more correction without clip if diff small.
```
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 0:
       h += diff / n
       # if small, clip? if clip changes sum, repeat 2
```
But adding diff/n may violate bounds by tiny 1e-16; clip fine. We'll do a couple.

If v has NaN due FFT? check in functions: if not np.isfinite(v).mean(): return previous? In run, guard try/except.

FFT functions:
```
def corr_full_scaled(h,n):
    L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    g=1.0-h
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(g[::-1],N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:L]
    c *= 2.0/n
    return c
```
Possible if L=1? n>0.
`c5_value`: return float(np.max(corr_full_scaled(h,n)))
`c5_direct`: use np.correlate for final.
`smooth_value(h,n,alpha)`: c=corr_full_scaled; m=float(c.max()); y=alpha*(c-m); e=np.exp(y); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: s=1; return m + np.log(s)/alpha
`smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha)`: as above. Need `np.log(s)` if s 0? impossible.

```
fw = np.fft.rfft(w,N)
A = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.fft.rfft(g,N), N)[n-1:2*n-1]
wn = w[::-1]
B = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(wn,N) * np.fft.rfft(h,N), N)[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Adam:
```
def adam_opt(h,n,alpha_schedule,max_steps,deadline,rng,base_stage=False):
    h = project_fast(h,n)
    current_c = c5_value(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = current_c
    for alpha, passes, max_step in alpha_schedule:
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       sm=np.zeros(n); sv=np.zeros(n); t=0; stall=0
       for _ in range(passes):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          obj,grad,c_arr,m = smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha)
          h_finite = np.all(np.isfinite(h))
          if not h_finite or not np.isfinite(obj): break
          current_c = m
          if current_c < best_c:
             best_c=current_c; best_h=h.copy()
          t+=1
          sm = 0.9*sm + 0.1*grad
          sv = 0.999*sv + 0.001*grad*grad
          mhat = sm/(1-0.9**t)
          vhat = sv/(1-0.999**t)
          update = mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
          update -= update.mean()
          norm = np.linalg.norm(update)
          if norm < 1e-14:
             break
          update *= max_step / norm
          d = -update
          accepted=False
          # Try scales maybe 1,0.5,0.25
          for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
             trial = project_fast(h + scale*d, n)
             f = c5_value(trial,n)
             if f < current_c - 1e-14:
                 h = trial; current_c=f; accepted=True; break
             if abs(f-current_c) <= 1e-12:
                 # evaluate smooth maybe
                 if smooth_value(trial,n,alpha) < obj - 1e-10:
                    h=trial; current_c=f; accepted=True; break
          if accepted:
             if current_c < best_c: ...
             stall=0
          else:
             stall += 1
             if stall > 6:
                noise = 0.01 * max(1.0, (alpha/1000.0)**(-0.5)?) 
                h = project_fast(best_h + rng.normal(0, 0.01/n? , n), n) # local noise std 0.02
                h = project_fast(best_h + 0.02*rng.randn(n),n)
                current_c = c5_value(h,n)
                # reset adam
                sm=np.zeros(n); sv=np.zeros(n); t=0; stall=0
    return best_h,best_c
```
Noise std: h vector values; noise 0.01 maybe too small, 0.05. Use `0.03`. But from best, perturb random smooth (white noise high freq) may not help; use low-frequency noise: generate random coefficients in basis? Simpler: white noise projected. But white noise may create bad local. Maybe skip noise; use different α schedules.
Could use `h_pert = project_fast(best_h + rng.normal(0,0.02,n), n)`.

Each iteration maybe 3 FFT for grad + 1-3 c5 (2 FFT) => ~7 FFT. 370*7=2590 FFT 2048 = ok. Time maybe 20s? Actually each FFT Python overhead maybe 0.0005, total 1.3s? Maybe more 10s. Good.
But `smooth_gradient` does 5 rffts+3 irffts. 370*5=1850 rfft; c5 1000*2=2000; total 4000 transforms. 2048 real FFT maybe ~0.1ms? 0.4s; overhead maybe 2s. Fine. Could increase.

Now low-dimensional optimizer with L-BFGS-B. Need ensure not too slow. We'll use it after Adam.
```
def build_basis(n, max_cols=16):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    cols=[]
    # low-degree Chebyshev 1..8
    xm=x-1.0
    Tprev=np.ones_like(x); T=xm.copy(); cols.append(T)
    for m in range(2,9):
       Tnew=2*xm*T - Tprev
       cols.append(Tnew)
       Tprev,T=T,Tnew
    # Fourier period 2 modes 1..3
    for m in range(1,4):
       cols.append(np.cos(m*np.pi*x))
       cols.append(np.sin(m*np.pi*x))
    # Normalize and remove tiny
    B_list=[]
    for c in cols:
       nrm=np.linalg.norm(c)
       if nrm>1e-8: B_list.append(c/nrm)
    # Remove near duplicate? optional
    B=np.column_stack(B_list)
    # Optional orthonormalize quickly with QR (if n>k)
    Q,R=np.linalg.qr(B)
    B=Q[:,:B.shape[1]]
    # ensure no large constant component? subtract mean and project? QR may include constant if columns have mean; Cheb T0 skipped but cos have mean; we want z mean not constrained? Mean handled by projection, but zero-mean basis better.
    B = B - B.mean(axis=0)
    # renormalize?
```
QR may produce columns with constant? We subtract mean. But if we subtract after QR, columns no longer orthonormal; objective still fine, coefficients scale. Could orthonormalize after centering with SVD? Simpler: Build centered columns and orthonormal via QR:
 for c: c = c - c.mean(); norm.
 Then QR. Good.
Cheb T_m (m≥1) may have mean zero over [0,2]? On sample discrete maybe close. Cos modes have nonzero mean; center them: c-mean. Fourier after centering becomes cos minus average over grid; still same span except constant. Fine.
Set K = min(12, len(cols))? To keep L-BFGS dims modest. Use K=12: Cheb 1..7 (7) + cos/sin 1,2? Actually 7+4=11. Use K=12 maybe add m=3 cos? Let's set Cheb 1..8 (8) + cos/sin mode1,2 (4) =>12. Good.

def build_basis(n):

Max g(x)=max(2x(1-x), x^2, (1-x)^2). At x=0.6, g=0.48, x^2=0.36. To reduce max, increase x? x=0.7: c0=0.42, x^2=0.49 -> max .49 worse. x=0.4: c0=0.48, (1-x)^2=0.36, max .48. x=0.5: .5. def make_sym_full(y,n):
 m=n//2
 h=np.empty(n)
 h[:m]=y

def sym_full(y,n):
 m=n//2
 h=np.empty(n)
 h[:m]=y
 if n%2==1: h[m]=0.5; start=m+1
 else: start=m
 h[start:]=1.0 - y[::-1]
 return h
```

Implement:
```
def build_basis_values(x, max_cols):
    minx=x.min(); maxx=x.max(); if maxx-minx<1e-12: xm=zeros
    else xm=2*(x-minx)/(maxx-minx)-1
    cols=[]
    Tprev=np.ones_like(x); T=xm.copy(); cols.append(T)
    for mdeg in range(2, max_cols+1): ...
    # center and normalize
    Blist=[]
    for c in cols: c=c-c.mean(); nrm=norm; if nrm>... append
    if not Blist: return np.zeros((len(x),0))
    B=np.column_stack(Blist)
    Q,R=np.linalg.qr(B)
    return Q[:, :min(max_cols,len(x))]
```
For sym, x = np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False). For full, x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False). Use max_cols maybe 10 for sym, 12 for full. If m small, count min.

Sym Adam:
```
def sym_adam(y,n,alphas,deadline,rng):
    m=n//2
    if m<=0: h=sym_full...
    y=np.clip(np.asarray(y,float),0,1)
    best_y=y.copy(); best_h=sym_full_from_y(y,n); best_c=c5_value(best_h,n)
    cur_y=y.copy(); cur_c=best_c
    for alpha,passes,max_step in alphas:
       sm=np.zeros(m); sv=np.zeros(m); t=0; stall=0
       for _ in range(passes):
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         h=sym_full_from_y(cur_y,n)
         obj,gh,c_arr,mval=smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha)
         if not np.isfinite(mval): break
         cur_c=mval
         if cur_c<best_c: best_c=cur_c; best_y=cur_y.copy(); best_h=h.copy()
         jidx=n-1-np.arange(m)
         gy=gh[:m]-gh[jidx]
         t+=1; update...
         if norm<1e-13: stall++;...
         accepted=False
         for scale in (1,0.5,0.25,0.125):
            ty=np.clip(cur_y+scale*d,0,1)
            th=sym_full_from_y(ty,n)
            f=c5_value(th,n)
            allow=False
            if f<cur_c-1e-14: allow=True
            elif f<cur_c+1e-4:
                obj_t=smooth_value(th,n,alpha)
                if obj_t<obj-1e-10: allow=True
            if allow:
                cur_y=ty; cur_c=f; accepted=True; if f<best_c update; break
         if not accepted:
             stall+=1
             if stall>8:
                cur_y=np.clip(best_y+rng.normal(0,0.02,m),0,1)
                cur_c=c5_value(sym_full_from_y(cur_y,n),n)
                # maybe if perturbation worse, still current; state reset
                sm.fill(0); sv.fill(0); t=0; stall=0
    return best_h,best_c
```

Implementation:
```
def state_full(z,n,mode):
    if mode == 0:
        return project_fast(z,n)
    return sym_full_from_y(z,n)

def state_grad(z,n,mode,alpha):
    h=state_full(z,n,mode)
    obj, gh, c, mval = smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha)
    if mode == 0:
        gh = gh - gh.mean()
        return obj, gh, mval
    m=n//2
    j=n-1-np.arange(m)
    gh = gh[:m] - gh[j]
    return obj, gh, mval

def state_apply(z,n,mode):
    if mode==0: return project_fast(z,n)
    return sym_full_from_y(z,n)
```

def lowdim_opt(h,n,mode,rng,deadline):
    if mode == 0:
        z=h
        B=build_basis_values(np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False),12)
    else:
        m=n//2
        if m<=2: return h,c5_value(h,n)
        z=sym_y_from_h(h,n)
        B=build_basis_values(np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False),10)
    if B.shape[1]==0: return h,c5_value(h,n)
    a=np.clip(B.T @ (z-0.5), -1.0, 1.0)
    bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)]*B.shape[1]
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    maxit=max(10,min(80,int(rem/2.0)))? Need estimate eval time 0.02? Use int(rem/5) maybe. If rem 200, maxit 40. Good.
    try:
       res=minimize(lowdim_objective,a,args=(B,n,mode),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,
                    options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-6})
       znp=0.5+B@res.x
       znp=np.clip(znp,0,1)
    except Exception:
       znp=z
    if mode==0: hnew=project_fast(znp,n); cnew=c5_value(hnew,n)
    else: hnew=sym_full_from_y(znp,n); cnew=c5_value(hnew,n)
    return hnew,cnew
```
But `lowdim_objective` for mode full uses project inside; mode sym uses sym. It takes a,B,n,mode. It calls c5_value. Good.
`maxit` based on remaining. If remaining 100, int(20) =>20. To avoid too little, use max(20,min(80,int(rem/3))). Each iteration ~13 eval; 20*13=260 eval maybe 2s. OK.
If mode sym, z length m; B length m. For full, z length n.
When mode=0 and n large, B T@(z-0.5) maybe OK.

`build_basis_values` details:
```
def build_basis_values(x,max_cols):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if x.size < max_cols: max_cols=max(1,x.size//2?)
    minx=x.min(); maxx=x.max()
    if maxx-minx > 1e-12: xm=2*(x-minx)/(maxx-minx)-1
    else: xm=np.zeros_like(x)
    cols=[]
    prev=np.ones_like(x); cur=xm.copy(); cols.append(cur)
    for deg in range(2,max_cols+1):
       new=2*xm*cur - prev
       cols.append(new); prev,cur=cur,new
    Blist=[]
    for c in cols:
       c=c-c.mean()
       nrm=np.linalg.norm(c)
       if nrm>1e-8: Blist.append(c/nrm)
       if len(Blist)>=max_cols: break
    if not Blist: return np.zeros((len(x),0))
    B=np.column_stack(Blist)
    Q,R=np.linalg.qr(B)
    return Q[:, :B.shape[1]]
```
Maybe too many Cheb modes; high-degree oscillatory. OK.
For sym basis domain [0,1], Cheb on [0,1]. Good.

Subtracting mean is projection onto sum-zero subspace. Chain on projected gradient equals chain on original? Because constant subtract cancels in pairs, yes. For center odd? no. Good.

Detailed code:
```
def project_fast(v, n):
    v = np.array(v, dtype=float, copy=True)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(20):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * room / tot
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += diff * h / tot
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(3):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        h += diff / n
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
The final `h += diff/n` may not preserve sum if clip changes; last diff maybe. Fine.

`sym_full_from_y`:
```
def sym_full_from_y(y, n):
    m = n // 2
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = y
    if n % 2:
        h[m] = 0.5
        h[m+1:] = 1.0 - y[::-1]
    else:
        h[m:] = 1.0 - y[::-1]
    return h
```
If m=0, h[:0], if n=1 h[0]=0.5. OK.

`sym_y_from_h`:
```
def sym_y_from_h(h,n):
 m=n//2
 if m==0: return np.array([])
 idx=np.arange(m)
 y=0.5*(h[idx] + 1.0 - h[n-1-idx])
 return np.clip(y,0,1)
```

`smooth_value` and `smooth_gradient`:
```
def smooth_value(h,n,alpha):
 c=corr_scaled_fft(h,n)
 m=float(np.max(c))
 y=alpha*(c-m)
 e=np.exp(y)
 s=float(np.sum(e))
 if s <= 0: s=1
 return m + np.log(s)/alpha
```
`corr_scaled_fft` uses if h not finite? maybe.
```
def corr_scaled_fft(h,n):
 L=2*n-1
 N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
 g=1.0-h
 fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
 fg=np.fft.rfft(g[::-1],N)
 c=np.fft.irfft(fh*fg,N)[:L]
 return c*(2.0/n)
```
Maybe `g[::-1]` is view; rfft of non-contiguous maybe slower. Could use `np.ascontiguousarray(g[::-1])`. Not necessary.

`smooth_gradient`:
```
def smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha):
 L=2*n-1; N=...
 g=1.0-h
 fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fg=np.fft.rfft(g,N); fgr=np.fft.rfft(g[::-1],N)
 c=np.fft.irfft(fh*fgr,N)[:L]*(2.0/n)
 m=float(np.max(c))
 y=alpha*(c-m)
 e=np.exp(y); s=float(np.sum(e)); w=e/s
 fw=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
 A=np.fft.irfft(fw*fg,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
 fwn=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],N)
 B=np.fft.irfft(fwn*fh,N)[n-1:2*n-1]
 grad=(2.0/n)*(A-B)
 grad -= np.mean(grad)
 obj=m+np.log(s)/alpha
 return obj, grad, c, m
```
Need if s==0? no.
If `np.max(c)` nan? fallback. In `adam_state`, if not finite, break. If `obj` nan return? Add:
```
if not np.isfinite(obj): return obj, np.zeros(n), c, m
```
But then m nan. In smooth_gradient, if not finite, set grad zeros, m = c5_value direct? Simpler assume finite. But protect:
```
if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e10, np.zeros(n), c, 1e10
```
But c may nan. In run, if not finite, break.

`lowdim_objective`:
```
def lowdim_objective(a, B, n, mode):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return 1e6
    z = 0.5 + B @ a
    z = np.clip(z,0,1)
    if mode == 0:
       h = project_fast(z,n)
    else:
       h = sym_full_from_y(z,n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return 1e6
    return c5_value(h,n)
```
If mode 1, z length must m. Good.

`lowdim_opt` after minimize: `znp` length. For mode 1, `z` from sym_y used for a. Good.

`adam_state`:
```
def adam_state(z,n,mode,alphas,deadline,rng):
    z=np.asarray(z,float).copy()
    if mode==0:
        z=project_fast(z,n)
    else:
        z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    current_h = state_full(z,n,mode) # maybe define state_full
    best_h = current_h.copy()
    best_c = c5_value(best_h,n)
    cur_z=z.copy(); cur_c=best_c
    dim = n if mode==0 else n//2
    for alpha,passes,max_step in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        sm=np.zeros(dim); sv=np.zeros(dim); t=0; stall=0
        for it in range(passes):
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            h=state_full(cur_z,n,mode)
            obj, gh, c_arr, mval = smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha)
            if not np.isfinite(mval): break
            if mode==0:
                g = gh - np.mean(gh)
            else:
                m=n//2; j=n-1-np.arange(m); g=gh[:m]-gh[j]
            cur_c=float(mval)
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c=cur_c; best_h=h.copy(); best_z=cur_z.copy()
            t+=1
            sm=0.9*sm+0.1*g
            sv=0.999*sv+0.001*g*g
            mhat=sm/(1-0.9**t); vhat=sv/(1-0.999**t)
            update=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
            if mode==0: update -= update.mean()
            norm=float(np.linalg.norm(update))
            if norm < 1e-13:
                if stall < 20: stall += 1
                else: restart...
                continue
            d = -update * (max_step/norm)
            accepted=False
            for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
                raw=cur_z+scale*d
                if mode==0:
                   trial_z=project_fast(raw,n); trial_h=trial_z
                else:
                   trial_z=np.clip(raw,0,1); trial_h=sym_full_from_y(trial_z,n)
                f=c5_value(trial_h,n)
                allow = f < cur_c - 1e-14
                if not allow and f < cur_c + 1e-4:
                    if smooth_value(trial_h,n,alpha) < obj - 1e-10:
                       allow=True
                if allow:
                   cur_z=trial_z; cur_c=f
                   if cur_c < best_c: update best
                   accepted=True; break
            if not accepted:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 6:
                   noise = 0.02
                   raw=best_z + rng.normal(0,noise,dim)
                   if mode==0: cur_z=project_fast(raw,n)
                   else: cur_z=np.clip(raw,0,1)
                   cur_h=state_full(cur_z,n,mode); cur_c=c5_value(cur_h,n)
                   sm.fill(0); sv.fill(0); t=0; stall=0
    return best_h,best_c,best_z? 
```

 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3.0

 # init
 ...
 best_h=...; best_c=...

def sym_coord_polish(y,n,passes,deadline,rng):
    m=n//2
    cur_y=np.clip(y,0,1); cur_h=sym_full_from_y(cur_y,n); cur_c=c5_value(cur_h,n)
    best_y=cur_y.copy(); best_c=cur_c; best_h=cur_h.copy()
    for p in range(passes):
       order = list(range(m)); if p>0: rng.shuffle(order)
       for i in order:
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         x=cur_y[i]
         cand0=x
         cand=[cand0, 0.0, 1.0, x-0.02, x+0.02, x-0.1, x+0.1]
         best_x=x; best_c_i=cur_c
         for vc in cand:
           if not (0<=vc<=1): continue
           if abs(vc-best_x)<1e-8? but we want min. Evaluate if abs(vc-x)>1e-7 maybe.
           trial_y=cur_y.copy(); trial_y[i]=vc
           th=sym_full_from_y(trial_y,n); fc=c5_value(th,n)
           if fc < best_c_i - 1e-15:
              best_c_i=fc; best_x=vc; h=th
         if best_x != x:
            cur_y[i]=best_x; cur_c=best_c_i
            if cur_c<best_c: update
    return best_h,best_c

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_fast(v,n):
    h=np.clip(np.array(v,dtype=float,copy=True),0.0,1.0)
    t=0.5*n
    for _ in range(12):
        d=t-h.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-13: break
        if d>0:
            r=1.0-h; s=r.sum()
            if s<=1e-15: break
            h += d*r/s
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<=1e-15: break
            h += d*h/s
        h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    d=t-h.sum()
    if abs(d)>1e-14:
        h=np.clip(h+d/n,0,0? no 1.0)
        h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
Note `np.array(v,...,copy=True)` with v maybe array. Good.
`h+d/n` line: use 1.0. Good.

Let's write adam_state compact:
```
def adam_state(z,n,mode,alphas,deadline,rng):
    z=np.asarray(z,float).copy()
    z=project_fast(z,n) if mode==0 else np.clip(z,0,1)
    cur=z.copy(); best=cur.copy()
    cur_h=state_full(cur,n,mode); cur_c=c5_value(cur_h,n)
    best_h=cur_h.copy(); best_c=cur_c
    dim=n if mode==0 else n//2
    for alpha,passes,mlen in alphas:
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        sm=np.zeros(dim); sv=np.zeros(dim); t=0; stall=0
        for _ in range(passes):
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            h=state_full(cur,n,mode)
            obj,gh,_,cmax=smooth_gradient(h,n,alpha)
            if not np.isfinite(cmax): break
            cur_c=float(cmax)
            if mode==0:
                g=gh-np.mean(gh)
            else:
                m=n//2; j=n-1-np.arange(m)
                g=gh[:m]-gh[j]
            if cur_c<best_c: best_c=cur_c; best=cur.copy(); best_h=h.copy()
            t+=1
            sm=0.9*sm+0.1*g; sv=0.999*sv+0.001*g*g
            mhat=sm/(1-0.9**t); vhat=sv/(1-0.999**t)
            up=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
            if mode==0: up-=np.mean(up)
            no=float(np.linalg.norm(up))
            acc=False
            if no>1e-13:
                d=-up*(mlen/no)
                for sc in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
                    raw=cur+sc*d
                    tz=project_fast(raw,n) if mode==0 else np.clip(raw,0,1)
                    th=state_full(tz,n,mode)
                    f=c5_value(th,n)
                    ok=f<cur_c-1e-14
                    if not ok and f<cur_c+1e-4 and np.isfinite(f):
                        if smooth_value(th,n,alpha)<obj-1e-10: ok=True
                    if ok:
                        cur=tz; cur_c=f; acc=True
                        if cur_c<best_c: best_c=cur_c; best=cur.copy(); best_h=th.copy()
                        break
            if not acc:
                stall += 10 if no<=1e-13 else 1
                if stall>8:
                    ns=0.015 if mode==1 else 0.01
                    raw=best+rng.normal(0.0,ns,dim)
                    cur=project_fast(raw,n) if mode==0 else np.clip(raw,0,1)
                    cur_h=state_full(cur,n,mode); cur_c=c5_value(cur_h,n)
                    sm.fill(0); sv.fill(0); t=0; stall=0
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def lowdim_opt(h,n,mode,rng,deadline):
    if mode==0:
       z=h; B=build_basis(np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False),10)
    else:
       m=n//2
       if m<3: return h,c5_value(h,n)
       z=sym_y_from_h(h,n); B=build_basis(np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False),8)
    if B.shape[1]==0: return h,c5_value(h,n)
    a=np.clip(B.T.dot(z-0.5),-1,1)
    bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)]*B.shape[1]
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem<5: return h,c5_value(h,n)
    maxit=max(10,min(50,int(rem/5.0)))
    try:
       res=minimize(lowdim_obj,a,args=(B,n,mode),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-6})
       zn=0.5+B@res.x
    except Exception:
       zn=z
    zn=np.clip(zn,0,1)
    hnew=project_fast(zn,n) if mode==0 else sym_full_from_y(zn,n)
    cnew=c5_value(hnew,n)
    if not np.isfinite(cnew): return h,c5_value(h,n)
    return hnew,cnew
```
`B@res.x` if res.x shape. Good.
`int(rem/5)` if rem 200 ->40; if each eval 0.01*11*40=4.4s. Good.

`build_basis` compact:
```
def build_basis(x,k):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    if x.size==0: return np.zeros((0,0))
    k=min(k,max(1,x.size//4))
    mn,xmax=x.min(),x.max()
    xm=2*(x-mn)/(xmax-mn)-1 if xmax-mn>1e-12 else np.zeros_like(x)
    cols=[]; tp=np.ones_like(x); tc=xm.copy(); cols.append(tc)
    for _ in range(2,k+1):
        tn=2*xm*tc-tp; cols.append(tn); tp,tc=tc,tn
    out=[]
    for c in cols:
        c=c-c.mean(); nr=np.linalg.norm(c)
        if nr>1e-8: out.append(c/nr)
        if len(out)>=k: break
    if not out: return np.zeros((len(x),0))
    B=np.column_stack(out)
    Q,R=np.linalg.qr(B)
    return Q[:,:B.shape[1]]
```
For x length 350, k=8. Good. If x.size<4, k=1.
Cheb degree 1..k; if k=8, cols 8.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); dl=t0+budget_s-3
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 try:
   arr=np.asarray(initial_h_values,float)
   if arr.size<8: raise ValueError
   h0=arr.copy(); n=arr.size
 except Exception:
   n=700; x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False); h0=np.clip(0.5+0.25*np.sin(np.pi*x)+0.1*np.sin(2*np.pi*x),0,1)
 if n>1000:
   no,n=1000,n
   x0=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False); x1=np.linspace(0,2,no,endpoint=False)
   h0=np.interp(x1,x0,h0); n=no
 h0=project_fast(h0,n)
 best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=c5_direct(h0,n)
 if n<3 or time.time()>dl-20: return best_h,min(best_c,1e6),n
 y=sym_y_from_h(h0,n)
 hs=sym_full_from_y(y,n); cs=c5_value(hs,n)
 if cs<best_c: best_h,best_c=hs,cs
 # sym medium
 if time.time()<dl-260:
   h1,c1=adam_state(y,n,1,A_MED,dl-220,rng)
   if c1<best_c: best_h,best_c=h1,c1
 if time.time()<dl-140:
   h2,c2=lowdim_opt(best_h,n,1,rng,dl-120)
   if c2<best_c: best_h,best_c=h2,c2
 if time.time()<dl-120:
   y=sym_y_from_h(best_h,n)
   h3,c3=adam_state(y,n,1,A_HIGH,dl-80,rng)
   if c3<best_c: best_h,best_c=h3,c3
 if time.time()<dl-90:
   h4,c4=lowdim_opt(best_h,n,0,rng,dl-70)
   if c4<best_c: best_h,best_c=h4,c4
 if time.time()<dl-80:

   if c5v<best_c: best_h,best_c=h5,c5v
 if time.time()<dl-50:
   m=n//2; B=build_basis(np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False),8) if m>4 else None
   if B is not None:
      for sc in (0.1,0.25,0.5):
         if time.time()>dl-25: break
         ar=rng.normal(0.0,sc,B.shape[1])
         yr=np.clip(0.5+B.dot(ar),0,1)
         h6,c6=adam_state(yr,n,1,A_SHORT,dl-25,rng)
         if c6<best_c: best_h,best_c=h6,c6
 if time.time()<dl-10:
   y=sym_y_from_h(best_h,n)
   h7,c7=adam_state(y,n,1,A_FINAL,dl,rng)
   if c7<best_c: best_h,best_c=h7,c7
 best_h=project_fast(best_h,n)
 cf=c5_direct(best_h,n)
 if not np.isfinite(cf): cf=best_c
 return best_h,cf,n

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_fast(v, n):
    h = np.clip(np.array(v, dtype=float, copy=True), 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(12):
        d = target - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += d * room / s
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s <= 1e-15:
                break
            h += d * h / s
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    d = target - h.sum()
    if abs(d) > 1e-14:
        h = np.clip(h + d / n, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h

def sym_full_from_y(y, n):
    m = n // 2
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    h[:m] = y
    if n % 2:
        h[m] = 0.5
        h[m + 1:] = 1.0 - y[::-1]
    else:
        h[m:] = 1.0 - y[::-1]
    return h

def sym_y_from_h(h, n):
    m = n // 2
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=float)
    idx = np.arange(m)
    y = 0.5 * (h[idx] + 1.0 - h[n - 1 - idx])
    return np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)

def state_full(z, n, mode):
    if mode == 0:
        return np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    return sym_full_from_y(z, n)

def corr_scaled_fft(h, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    g = 1.0 - h
    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    fg = np.fft.rfft(g[::-1], N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fh * fg, N)[:L]
    return c * (2.0 / n)

def c5_value(h, n):
    return float(np.max(corr_scaled_fft(h, n)))

def c5_direct(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def smooth_value(h, n, alpha):
    c = corr_scaled_fft(h, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e9
    e = np.exp(alpha * (c - m))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    return m + np.log(max(s, 1e-300)) / alpha

def smooth_gradient(h, n, alpha):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    g = 1.0 - h
    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    fg = np.fft.rfft(g, N)
    fgr = np.fft.rfft(g[::-1], N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fh * fgr, N)[:L] * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e9, np.zeros(n, dtype=float), c, 1e9
    e = np.exp(alpha * (c - m))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0:
        s = 1.0
    w = e / s
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    A = np.fft.irfft(fw * fg, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    fwn = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    B = np.fft.irfft(fwn * fh, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    grad = (2.0 / n) * (A - B)
    obj = m + np.log(max(s, 1e-300)) / alpha
    return obj, grad, c, m

def build_basis(x, k):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.zeros((0, 0))
    k = int(min(k, max(1, x.size // 4)))
    mn = float(x.min())
    mx = float(x.max())
    xm = 2.0 * (x - mn) / (mx - mn) - 1.0 if mx - mn > 1e-12 else np.zeros_like(x)
    cols = []
    tp = np.ones_like(x)
    tc = xm.copy()
    cols.append(tc)
    for _ in range(1, k):
        tn = 2.0 * xm * tc - tp
        cols.append(tn)
        tp, tc = tc, tn
    out = []
    for c in cols:
        c = c - c.mean()
        nr = float(np.linalg.norm(c))
        if nr > 1e-8:
            out.append(c / nr)
        if len(out) >= k:
            break
    if not out:
        return np.zeros((len(x), 0))
    B = np.column_stack(out)
    Q, _ = np.linalg.qr(B)
    return Q[:, :B.shape[1]]

def lowdim_objective(a, B, n, mode):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return 1e9
    z = 0.5 + B @ a
    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    if mode == 0:
        h = project_fast(z, n)
    else:
        h = sym_full_from_y(z, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e9
    return c5_value(h, n)

def lowdim_opt(h, n, mode, rng, deadline):
    if mode == 0:
        z = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
        B = build_basis(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False), 10)
    else:
        m = n // 2
        if m < 3:
            return h, c5_value(h, n)
        z = sym_y_from_h(h, n)
        B = build_basis(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False), 8)
    if B.shape[1] == 0:
        return h, c5_value(h, n)
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 5.0:
        return h, c5_value(h, n)
    a0 = np.clip(B.T @ (z - 0.5), -1.0, 1.0)
    bounds = [(-1.0, 1.0)] * B.shape[1]
    maxit = int(max(10, min(50, rem / 6.0)))
    try:
        res = minimize(lowdim_objective, a0, args=(B, n, mode), method='L-BFGS-B',
                       bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol': 1e-10, 'gtol': 1e-6})
        zn = 0.5 + B @ res.x
    except Exception:
        zn = z
    zn = np.clip(np.asarray(zn, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    if mode == 0:
        hnew = project_fast(zn, n)
    else:
        hnew = sym_full_from_y(zn, n)
    cnew = c5_value(hnew, n)
    if not np.isfinite(cnew):
        return h, c5_value(h, n)
    return hnew, cnew

def adam_state(z, n, mode, alphas, deadline, rng):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).copy()
    z = project_fast(z, n) if mode == 0 else np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    cur = z.copy()
    best = z.copy()
    cur_h = state_full(cur, n, mode)
    cur_c = c5_value(cur_h, n)
    best_h = cur_h.copy()
    best_c = cur_c
    dim = n if mode == 0 else n // 2
    if dim == 0:
        return best_h, best_c
    for alpha, passes, mlen in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        sm = np.zeros(dim, dtype=float)
        sv = np.zeros(dim, dtype=float)
        t = 0
        stall = 0
        for _ in range(passes):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h = state_full(cur, n, mode)
            obj, gh, _, cmax = smooth_gradient(h, n, alpha)
            if not np.isfinite(cmax):
                break
            cur_c = float(cmax)
            if mode == 0:
                g = gh - np.mean(gh)
            else:
                m = n // 2
                j = n - 1 - np.arange(m)
                g = gh[:m] - gh[j]
            if cur_c < best_c:
                best_c = cur_c
                best = cur.copy()
                best_h = h.copy()
            t += 1
            sm = 0.9 * sm + 0.1 * g
            sv = 0.999 * sv + 0.001 * g * g
            mhat = sm / (1.0 - 0.9 ** t)
            vhat = sv / (1.0 - 0.999 ** t)
            up = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
            if mode == 0:
                up -= np.mean(up)
            no = float(np.linalg.norm(up))
            acc = False
            if no > 1e-13:
                d = -up * (mlen / no)
                for sc in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                    raw = cur + sc * d
                    tz = project_fast(raw, n) if mode == 0 else np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1.0)
                    th = state_full(tz, n, mode)
                    f = c5_value(th, n)
                    ok = f < cur_c - 1e-14
                    if not ok and f < cur_c + 1e-4 and np.isfinite(f):
                        if smooth_value(th, n, alpha) < obj - 1e-10:
                            ok = True
                    if ok:
                        cur = tz
                        cur_c = f
                        acc = True
                        if cur_c < best_c:
                            best_c = cur_c
                            best = cur.copy()
                            best_h = th.copy()
                        break
            if not acc:
                stall += 10 if no <= 1e-13 else 1
                if stall > 8:
                    ns = 0.015 if mode == 1 else 0.01
                    raw = best + rng.normal(0.0, ns, dim)
                    cur = project_fast(raw, n) if mode == 0 else np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1.0)
                    cur_h = state_full(cur, n, mode)
                    cur_c = c5_value(cur_h, n)
                    sm.fill(0.0)
                    sv.fill(0.0)
                    t = 0
                    stall = 0
    return best_h, best_c

A_MED = ((30, 30, 0.06), (100, 35, 0.04), (300, 45, 0.03),
         (1000, 55, 0.02), (4000, 60, 0.012), (15000, 70, 0.007))
A_HIGH = ((2000, 40, 0.015), (10000, 50, 0.010),
          (50000, 70, 0.006), (200000, 80, 0.004))
A_FULL = ((100, 25, 0.04), (500, 35, 0.025), (3000, 45, 0.015))
A_SHORT = ((50, 15, 0.05), (300, 25, 0.03), (1500, 35, 0.015))
A_FINAL = ((5000, 30, 0.008), (30000, 40, 0.005), (100000, 50, 0.003))

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if arr.size < 8 or not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            raise ValueError
        h0 = arr.copy()
        n = int(arr.size)
    except Exception:
        n = 700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        h0 = np.clip(0.5 + 0.25 * np.sin(np.pi * x) + 0.10 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.0, 1.0)

    if n > 1000:
        nn = 1000
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nn, endpoint=False)
        h0 = np.interp(x1, x0, h0)
        n = nn

    h0 = project_fast(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = c5_direct(h0, n)

    if n < 3 or time.time() > deadline - 15.0:
        return best_h, best_c, n

    y = sym_y_from_h(h0, n)
    hs = sym_full_from_y(y, n)
    cs = c5_value(hs, n)
    if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_c - 1e-12:
        best_h, best_c = hs, cs

    if time.time() < deadline - 260.0:
        h1, c1 = adam_state(y, n, 1, A_MED, deadline - 230.0, rng)
        if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h1, c1

    if time.time() < deadline - 140.0:
        h2, c2 = lowdim_opt(best_h, n, 1, rng, deadline - 120.0)
        if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        y = sym_y_from_h(best_h, n)
        h3, c3 = adam_state(y, n, 1, A_HIGH, deadline - 80.0, rng)
        if np.isfinite(c3) and c3 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h3, c3

    if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
        h4, c4 = lowdim_opt(best_h, n, 0, rng, deadline - 70.0)
        if np.isfinite(c4) and c4 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h4, c4

    if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
        h5, c5v = adam_state(best_h, n, 0, A_FULL, deadline - 50.0, rng)
        if np.isfinite(c5v) and c5v < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h5, c5v

    if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
        m = n // 2
        if m > 4:
            B = build_basis(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False), 8)
            for sc in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
                if time.time() > deadline - 25.0:
                    break
                ar = rng.normal(0.0, sc, B.shape[1])
                yr = np.clip(0.5 + B @ ar, 0.0, 1.0)
                h6, c6 = adam_state(yr, n, 1, A_SHORT, deadline - 25.0, rng)
                if np.isfinite(c6) and c6 < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_h, best_c = h6, c6

    if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        y = sym_y_from_h(best_h, n)
        h7, c7 = adam_state(y, n, 1, A_FINAL, deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(c7) and c7 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_c = h7, c7

    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = 0.5 * n - best_h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-8:
        best_h = project_fast(best_h, n)
    elif abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        interior = (best_h > 1e-12) & (best_h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if interior.any():
            best_h[interior] += diff / float(interior.sum())
            best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)

    final_c = c5_direct(best_h, n)
    if not np.isfinite(final_c):
        final_c = best_c
    return best_h, float(final_c), int(n)
```
